People keep saying it’s just normal oversight, but I can tell certain drivers are reporting on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think these comments on the radio are actually about you, or could they be about something else?\n\nPatient: I know they are about me. Like I said, it's not just random chatter. When I have to make a route change, the computer system logs it, and then almost right away I hear those little comments on the radio. \n\nPeople try to tell me it's just normal dispatch oversight, but... I know what I'm hearing. It's too specific to what I'm doing right at that exact moment. I can just tell that certain drivers out there are watching my truck and reporting my movements back to compliance. It couldn't be about anything else.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered the possibility that you might be misunderstanding these comments on the radio?\n\nPatient: I mean, people tell me that. They try to say it’s just normal oversight and that I'm taking it the wrong way. And I guess I’ve tried to look at it like that, but... no, I'm not misunderstanding it. \n\nLike I was just saying, dispatch and compliance are definitely tracking my route changes through the computer system. The little comments on the radio happen exactly when I do something. I can just tell that certain drivers are out there reporting on me. It’s too exact to be a mistake or a misunderstanding.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been thinking that certain drivers are reporting on you?\n\nPatient: It's been going on for a little while now. I'd say the tracking through the computer system and those radio comments... it's all felt really clear to me for about the past two months.\n\nDoctor: Have your thoughts about being tracked and reported on by certain drivers changed at all over the past two months?\n\nPatient: No, they haven't changed. If anything, I'm just more sure of it now. I mean, that's why I'm here. Dispatch and compliance have been tracking my route changes through the computer system, and then I hear those little comments on the radio. People keep telling me it's just normal oversight, but... I know it's not. I can just tell that certain drivers are out there reporting on me. It's been exactly like that this whole time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any periods, even briefly, where you didn't think certain drivers were reporting on you?\n\nPatient: No, not really. It's been constant. I mean, that's the whole reason I'm here. Dispatch and compliance have been tracking my route changes through the computer system the entire time. And those little comments on the radio... they never really stop. I know people keep trying to tell me it's just normal oversight, but I can tell certain drivers are reporting on me. There hasn't been a time where that wasn't happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's any possibility, no matter how small, that you're wrong about certain drivers reporting on you?\n\nPatient: No, I really don't think I'm wrong. I mean, I've said it before, but I'm here because dispatch and compliance have been tracking my route changes through the computer system. And I hear those little comments on the radio. It's just... it's too obvious. People keep saying it's normal oversight, but I can tell certain drivers are reporting on me. I just know they are.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever tried to gather evidence or proof that certain drivers are indeed reporting on you?\n\nPatient: I mean... I haven't really tried to collect hard proof, like recording them or printing things out. I haven't needed to. Like I keep saying, the proof is just right there in front of me. I know I sound like a broken record today, but I'm here because dispatch and compliance are tracking my route changes through the computer system. The evidence is those little comments I hear on the radio. Even when people try to brush it off and tell me it's just normal oversight, I can just tell certain drivers are reporting on me. I don't really need to gather evidence when it's that obvious.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief that certain drivers are reporting on you ever changed or wavered, even for a short time?\n\nPatient: No, it hasn't wavered at all. I know I'm repeating myself a lot today, but... the whole reason I'm here is because dispatch and compliance have been tracking my route changes through the computer system. That hasn't stopped. And those little comments on the radio keep happening. So even when people tell me it's just normal oversight, I can tell certain drivers are reporting on me. I've never doubted that for a second.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Like I said, it's not just random chatter. I know what I'm hearing. It's too specific to what I'm doing right at that exact moment. I can just tell that certain drivers out there are watching my truck and reporting my movements back to compliance. People keep saying it’s just normal oversight, but I can tell certain drivers are reporting on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think these comments on the radio are actually about you, or could they be about something else?\n\nPatient: I know they are about me. Like I said, it's not just random chatter. When I have to make a route change, the computer system logs it, and then almost right away I hear those little comments on the radio. \n\nPeople try to tell me it's just normal dispatch oversight, but... I know what I'm hearing. It's too specific to what I'm doing right at that exact moment. I can just tell that certain drivers out there are watching my truck and reporting my movements back to compliance. It couldn't be about anything else.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered the possibility that you might be misunderstanding these comments on the radio?\n\nPatient: I mean, people tell me that. They try to say it’s just normal oversight and that I'm taking it the wrong way. And I guess I’ve tried to look at it like that, but... no, I'm not misunderstanding it. \n\nLike I was just saying, dispatch and compliance are definitely tracking my route changes through the computer system. The little comments on the radio happen exactly when I do something. I can just tell that certain drivers are out there reporting on me. It’s too exact to be a mistake or a misunderstanding.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been thinking that certain drivers are reporting on you?\n\nPatient: It's been going on for a little while now. I'd say the tracking through the computer system and those radio comments... it's all felt really clear to me for about the past two months.\n\nDoctor: Have your thoughts about being tracked and reported on by certain drivers changed at all over the past two months?\n\nPatient: No, they haven't changed. If anything, I'm just more sure of it now. I mean, that's why I'm here. Dispatch and compliance have been tracking my route changes through the computer system, and then I hear those little comments on the radio. People keep telling me it's just normal oversight, but... I know it's not. I can just tell that certain drivers are out there reporting on me. It's been exactly like that this whole time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any periods, even briefly, where you didn't think certain drivers were reporting on you?\n\nPatient: No, not really. It's been constant. I mean, that's the whole reason I'm here. Dispatch and compliance have been tracking my route changes through the computer system the entire time. And those little comments on the radio... they never really stop. I know people keep trying to tell me it's just normal oversight, but I can tell certain drivers are reporting on me. There hasn't been a time where that wasn't happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's any possibility, no matter how small, that you're wrong about certain drivers reporting on you?\n\nPatient: No, I really don't think I'm wrong. I mean, I've said it before, but I'm here because dispatch and compliance have been tracking my route changes through the computer system. And I hear those little comments on the radio. It's just... it's too obvious. People keep saying it's normal oversight, but I can tell certain drivers are reporting on me. I just know they are.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever tried to gather evidence or proof that certain drivers are indeed reporting on you?\n\nPatient: I mean... I haven't really tried to collect hard proof, like recording them or printing things out. I haven't needed to. Like I keep saying, the proof is just right there in front of me. I know I sound like a broken record today, but I'm here because dispatch and compliance are tracking my route changes through the computer system. The evidence is those little comments I hear on the radio. Even when people try to brush it off and tell me it's just normal oversight, I can just tell certain drivers are reporting on me. I don't really need to gather evidence when it's that obvious.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief that certain drivers are reporting on you ever changed or wavered, even for a short time?\n\nPatient: No, it hasn't wavered at all. I know I'm repeating myself a lot today, but... the whole reason I'm here is because dispatch and compliance have been tracking my route changes through the computer system. That hasn't stopped. And those little comments on the radio keep happening. So even when people tell me it's just normal oversight, I can tell certain drivers are reporting on me. I've never doubted that for a second.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered that your belief about being tracked might be false, even if it feels true to you?\n\nPatient: I feel like we're just going in circles now. No, I haven't considered that it's false, because it isn't. \n\nI know I keep saying the exact same thing to you, but dispatch and compliance really are tracking my route changes through the computer system. I'm actually hearing those little comments on the radio. It doesn't just feel true to me, it is true. Even when people try to tell me it's just normal oversight, I can tell certain drivers are reporting on me. So... no, I don't think it's false.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other beliefs like this one that you later found out were not true?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I've never had anything like this happen before. I hear the little comments on the radio. People try to tell me it's just normal oversight, but I can tell certain drivers are reporting on me. I haven't had any other beliefs like this because this isn't just a belief, it's what's actually happening right now.\n\nDoctor: Has there ever been a time when you weren't thinking about being tracked by certain drivers?\n\nPatient: No, not really. Just this situation at work.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the belief that certain drivers are reporting on you is something that could happen to anyone, or is it something unusual or special that's happening to you?\n\nPatient: I think... well, I guess it could happen to anyone if they were in my position, but it's happening specifically to me right now.
